# Dan Kornas 2026: every tool promotion (parent tweet + GitHub self-reply)

Unique repositories promoted: **2105**
Coverage: Jan 1 - Nov 4, 2026. Source: his own X account (self-reply pattern),
harvested through the authenticated timeline/search GraphQL with the session
cookies, plus parent-tweet details per ID. Every pair is matched.
